The benchmark comprises approximately 1,240 question-answer pairs spanning three categories: boolean, numeric, and verbal. NuclearQAv2 is constructed using a hybrid pipeline that combines expert-authored questions, existing datasets, and LLM-assisted generation from domain-specific technical corpora. By leveraging structured prompting for both automated question generation and response evaluation, the proposed framework enables scalable benchmark construction and evaluation. We evaluate a diverse set of LLMs using NuclearQAv2 and observe substantial performance differences across task types. While the models generally perform well on factual questions, quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding remain considerably more challenging. These results highlight the importance of multi-faceted evaluation frameworks and establish NuclearQAv2 as a scalable benchmark for assessing LLM capabilities in technical domains.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Explainable Adjudicative Variance: Quantifying Judicial Discretion via Gated Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27069)
*Stanisław Sójka,Felix Steffek,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种法官感知的门控多任务学习架构，通过细粒度法律结果分类体系和动态门控机制，有效分离案件事实与司法裁量因素，在参数效率显著提升的同时，实现了法律结果预测的新基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律结果预测需区分客观案件事实与司法裁量背景，传统方法难以有效解耦这两类信息，导致模型在处理模糊/罕见案例时表现受限。现有基于提示的生成式方法存在参数冗余和上下文信号弱组合问题。

Method: 提出Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning架构：1) 设计细粒度结果分类体系作为结构正则化约束；2) 构建门控融合机制动态调节法官身份对预测的影响；3) 采用LoRA适配Gemma-4编码器实现高效微调。

Result: 在13,937个英国就业法庭案例上，该方法相比SFT基线：1) 参数量减少一个数量级；2) 在模糊/罕见类别准确率提升显著；3) 通过法官嵌入和校准曲线实现可解释性分析。

Conclusion: 结构化可微分条件接口设计比单纯扩大模型规模更有效，证明了架构创新对身份条件分类任务的关键作用，为法律AI系统提供了参数高效且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Legal outcome prediction must disentangle objective case facts from adjudicative context. Merit-based rulings rely on factual evidence while technical disposals may hinge on judicial discretion. We propose a Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning architecture that explicitly models this distinction. We introduce a fine-grained outcome taxonomy to supervise the encoder, enforcing a structural regularization that disentangles distinct semantic pathways. This granular legal curriculum enables our Gated Fusion mechanism to dynamically modulate reliance on judge identity. We evaluate our approach on 13,937 UK Employment Tribunal decisions. We benchmark our design against supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a Gemma-4 26B-A4B backbone, in which judge identity and the taxonomy are injected as prompt tokens or autoregressive output targets. The two contextual signals compose only weakly when forced through a single autoregressive channel. In contrast, coupling a LoRA-adapted Gemma-4 encoder with our gated architecture defines a new state of the art on this benchmark while requiring an order of magnitude fewer trainable parameters than the generative SFT baselines, with gains concentrated on the most ambiguous and rarest outcome classes. Beyond accuracy, the architecture is interpretable; learned judge embeddings and calibration profiles localize the cases where adjudicative context drives the prediction. These results indicate that, for identity-conditioned classification of legal outcomes, the choice of conditioning interface dominates scale: differentiable structured composition yields more accurate, more parameter-efficient models than prompt-based composition over a substantially larger backbone.

</details>


### [149] [The Riddle Riddle: Testing Flexible Reasoning in Large Language Models and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27103)
*Bella Fascendini,Kathryn McGregor,Max D. Gupta,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过创新的“谜语谜语范式”发现，大型语言模型（LLMs）在解决需灵活推理的任务时倾向于依赖表面模式匹配而非内容理解，其高准确率可能源于记忆检索而非真正的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLMs在认知任务中表现优异，但其准确性究竟是源于训练数据的模式匹配还是真正的灵活推理尚不明确。为区分这两种机制，需设计能暴露推理策略差异的新测试范式。

Method: 提出“谜语谜语范式”：构造与真实谜语结构相似但仅需字面解释的问题。通过对比LLMs和人类在真实谜语（需创造性推理）与谜语谜语（需字面推理）上的表现，分析其推理策略选择机制。

Result: 实验显示：LLMs在真实谜语上准确率显著高于谜语谜语（84.9% vs 50.7%），而人类表现相反（50.5% vs 80.5%）。错误分析表明，LLMs 90.8%的错误源于不当使用创造性推理，而人类仅57.6%错误因过度字面化。

Conclusion: LLMs在看似需要推理的任务中的表现可能反映记忆检索而非灵活策略选择。当缺乏针对性刺激设计时，易将LLMs生成的类推理输出误判为真实推理能力，凸显当前评估范式的局限性。

Abstract: Humans flexibly adapt their reasoning strategies to the requirements of a given problem. Large language models (LLMs) have performed well on many cognitive tasks, however, it is unclear whether this accuracy is a result of pattern matching from training data or flexible reasoning. Here, we introduce a novel paradigm to test this question: the riddle riddle paradigm. Riddle riddles are word problems written to mimic popular riddles, but altered so their answers only require literal interpretations. Identifying correct answers requires looking past the structure of each question and flexibly apply different reasoning strategies based on the content. If LLMs respond to surface features, such as form, a riddle-like structure should cause models to use an inventive reasoning strategy even when a literal interpretation suffices. Alternatively, if LLMs reason based on content, they should flexibly switch strategies when appropriate. Across two experiments with nine state-of-the-art LLMs and 100 human participants, we show humans and LLMs fail on this paradigm in opposite directions. LLMs were far more accurate on genuine riddles than on riddle riddles (84.9% vs. 50.7%); whereas humans showed the reverse effect (50.5% vs. 80.5%). Error analysis shows that 90.8% of LLM errors on riddle riddles (the condition where they show diminished performance) were due to inappropriate use of inventive reasoning while only 57.6% of human errors on genuine riddles were due to overextending literal reasoning. Thus, while both groups make mistakes, reasoning mistakes are made more often by LLMs than by humans. Overall, LLMs' strong performance on genuine riddles may reflect memory retrieval rather than flexible strategy selection, and without stimuli designed to elicit this contrast, it becomes easy to conflate LLM-generated outputs that look like reasoning with genuine reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [Forecasting With LLMs: Improved Generalization Through Feature Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27199)
*Humzah Merchant,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过稀疏自编码器分析LLMs在预测任务中的内部状态，发现时间感知特征可抑制前瞻偏差，提升基于历史的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLMs在预测任务中是否依赖时间特定知识或可推广模式，以优化其未来状态预测的可靠性。

Method: 将LLMs应用于多种预测任务，使用稀疏自编码器解析其内部状态特征，识别时间感知与前瞻偏差相关特征，并在不同领域进行特征干预实验。

Result: 增强时间感知特征显著降低前瞻偏差且保持推理性能，而调整前瞻偏差特征无效。

Conclusion: 可解释的时间特征能因果性引导LLMs转向历史锚定的推理模式，提升预测的泛化能力。

Abstract: Successful forecasting involves identifying patterns between historical and future states of the world which generalize to future observations. We apply LLMs to a variety of forecasting tasks and inspect their internal states using sparse autoencoders to understand whether they appear to rely on time-specific pieces of knowledge versus generalizable patterns. Our analyses identify features associated with both time-aware reasoning and look-ahead-biased reasoning. We then apply the LLMs to an entirely different domain and intervene on these features. We find that amplifying time-awareness features substantially reduces look-ahead bias on forecasting prompts while preserving general reasoning performance. In contrast, steering the candidate look-ahead-bias features does not produce an effect. These results suggest that interpretable temporal features can be used to causally shift LLMs toward more historically grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [151] [Syntactic Belief Update as the Driver of Garden Path Processing Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27206)
*Alan Zhou,Miloš Stanojević,John T. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出句法信念更新模型，通过广义Rényi散度衡量句法信念更新幅度，优于传统词汇惊讶度预测花园路径句处理难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇惊讶度无法有效预测花园路径句的处理难度，需探索非词汇依赖的替代指标。

Method: 提出句法信念更新模型，动态预测句法树概率分布并在每个新词后更新，使用广义Rényi散度量化更新幅度。

Result: 该指标在花园路径句的人类阅读时间数据上表现优于词汇惊讶度。

Conclusion: 支持开发纯非词汇的惊讶度替代指标，为心理语言学提供新研究方向。

Abstract: Garden path sentences present a processing difficulty for humans -- the sentence prefix leads the listener towards one interpretation, until the listener hears a critical word that shows that the initial interpretation was wrong. Lexical surprisal, a measure that usually predicts sentence processing difficulty quite well, fails to provide good predictions for garden path sentences.
  We propose an alternative that actively predicts a probability distribution over syntactic trees (its syntactic belief) and updates that distribution after each new word. If a processor is led down a garden path, syntactic beliefs will be wrong and will require a large update at the critical word. The magnitude of the update is measured with a generalized Rényi divergence. Crucially, this metric is dependent on lexical items, but is fully independent of the probability of lexical items. This Syntactic Belief Update provides a better fit to the human reading time data on garden path sentences. This suggests a new research direction examining purely non-lexical alternatives to surprisal for psycholinguistics.

</details>


### [152] [Paved with True Intents: Intent-Aware Training Improves LLM Safety Classification Across Training Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27210)
*Jeremias Ferrao,Niclas Müller-Hof,Iustin Sîrbu,Traian Rebedea,Yftah Ziser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出AIMS数据集（1,724条安全提示+意图描述+伤害标签），验证将用户意图作为显式信号建模可显著提升安全分类器性能。实验表明：意图条件蒸馏优于纯推理蒸馏，基于GRPO的意图忠实度奖励机制在5项外部基准测试中取得最佳平均性能，且模型在推理延迟-F1指标上达到帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全分类器缺乏对用户意图的显式建模，导致难以准确区分恶意提示与合法请求，影响安全性与可用性平衡。

Method: 构建人类标注的AIMS数据集（含意图描述和伤害标签），系统评估意图感知训练方法：监督微调(SFT)、偏好学习(DPO)、推理蒸馏及强化学习(GRPO)，重点研究意图忠实度奖励机制。

Result: 1) DPO通过修正模型生成的意图错误优于SFT；2) 意图条件蒸馏在多数师生模型对中超越纯推理蒸馏；3) GRPO直接奖励意图忠实度在5项外部安全基准上取得最高平均分；4) 意图感知模型形成推理延迟-F1帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 意图忠实度建模是一种紧凑且高质量的监督信号，能有效提升安全分类器的鲁棒性，在保持低延迟的同时实现更高安全性。

Abstract: We argue that safety classifiers should model user intent as an explicit signal between the prompt and the final label. To study this, we introduce AIMS, a human-annotated dataset of 1,724 difficult safety prompts, each paired with an intent description and harm label. We use AIMS to evaluate intent-aware training across supervised fine-tuning, preference learning, reasoning distillation, and reinforcement learning. Despite its size, AIMS enables competitive safety classifiers across training regimes: DPO from model-generated intent errors improves over SFT, and intent-conditioned distillation outperforms reasoning-only distillation in most teacher-student pairs. Most notably, directly rewarding intent faithfulness with GRPO yields the strongest average performance across five external safety benchmarks, while our intent-aware models form the inference latency-F1 Pareto frontier. These results show that faithful intent modeling is a compact, high-quality supervision signal for more robust safety classifiers.

</details>


### [153] [Compositionality and the lexicon in evolutionary semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27228)
*Fausto Carcassi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将形式语义学的递归组合观点整合到进化建模中，提出词汇意义与组合函数共进化框架，发现量化语义中的保守性作为高效系统级抽象出现，并验证了形式语义学与进化模型结合的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义普遍性研究存在局限：要么假设固定词汇结构，要么忽略可解释的词汇部分进行整体组合。形式语义学强调句子意义通过递归组合词汇意义产生，但这一核心观点未被充分融入进化建模，导致对语义普遍性（如量化意义）的解释不足。

Method: 构建词汇意义与组合函数在概念简洁性和交流准确性双重压力下共进化的框架，应用于量化意义的进化研究，通过帕累托前沿分析系统效率与语义结构的关联。

Result: 发现保守性（quantifier conservativity）作为系统级抽象自然涌现，该解释对句法结构敏感，成功调和量化可学习性实证证据与既往进化模型的矛盾，证明形式语义学框架可有效指导进化建模。

Conclusion: 形式语义学的句子意义理论可与进化建模有机结合，所提框架为研究语法范畴内全局压缩、句法论元语义特化及词汇-组合意义共进化等语义普遍性现象提供了可推广的分析模板。

Abstract: Formal semantics has shown that sentence meanings arise by recursively composing lexical meanings, yet much of the literature on semantic universals models either lexicons with fixed signal structures or holistic composition without interpretable lexical parts. We introduce a framework that integrates this fundamental insight of formal semantics in evolutionary modeling, by allowing lexical meanings and a composition function to co-evolve under pressures for conceptual simplicity and communicative accuracy. We apply this framework to the evolution of quantificational meaning. Analyzing the Pareto frontier, we find that the most well-known semantic universal, conservativity, emerges as an efficient system-wide abstraction. The account is sensitive to syntactic structure and helps reconcile tensions between empirical evidence on quantifier learnability and prior evolutionary models. More broadly, the results demonstrate that the picture of sentential meaning developed in formal semantics can be productively combined with evolutionary modeling. The framework offers a template for studying universals that involve global compression within a grammatical category, semantic specialization of syntactic arguments, and the co-evolution of lexical and compositional meaning.

</details>


### [154] [CARVE: Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency for Chunk-Parallel Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27229)
*Sayak Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARVE通过仅在键轴执行擦除操作解决GDN-2架构的三大缺陷，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升记忆能力与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环模型的门控机制存在"记忆盲区"问题：擦除决策仅依赖新输入token而忽略待修改的记忆内容，导致参数浪费且破坏WY型三角块求解器的有效性。

Method: 提出CARVE架构，核心创新包括：(1)严格限制擦除操作仅在键轴进行；(2)复用已写入GPU内存的循环输出张量作为内容感知信号；(3)用每头单标量替代逐值写门投影。初始化时与GDN-2完全一致，差异仅来自内容门学习。

Result: 在1.3B参数/100B token规模下：WikiText困惑度15.72(较GDN-2降低0.18,4.5σ显著性)；9项常识推理基准全面领先；RULER检索探针全维度SOTA；吞吐量仅增加0.4%，峰值内存减少13%，参数量降低19%。

Conclusion: 证明键轴专属擦除是维持WY求解器有效性的充要条件，通过理论定理(记忆容量/李雅普诺夫稳定性/梯度流等)和实证结果验证了架构优势，实现了循环模型与Transformer竞争力的突破。

Abstract: Recurrent models must forget in order to remember, yet the state of the art decides what to erase without consulting what is stored -- the gate sees only the arriving token, not the memory it is about to modify. This memory-blind gating is one of three coupled defects in the leading delta-rule architecture (GDN-2): the value-axis erase mask wastes parameters at the scale of the value projection, and -- as we prove -- mathematically prevents the WY-form triangular chunk solver that makes recurrent training competitive with Transformers.
  We introduce CARVE (Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency), which resolves all three problems through one principle: erase only on the key axis. This is provably necessary and sufficient for the WY-form solver to remain valid. Within it, CARVE reuses the recurrent output tensor -- already written to GPU memory -- as a free content signal for the erase gate, and replaces the per-value write-gate projection with a single scalar per head. At initialisation CARVE is bit-identical to GDN-2; any quality difference emerges from what the content gate learns.
  At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B tokens, CARVE achieves WikiText perplexity 15.72 (minus 0.18 vs. GDN-2, a 4.5-sigma effect), leads every recurrent baseline on nine common-sense reasoning benchmarks, and sets state of the art on every RULER retrieval probe -- at 0.4% throughput overhead, 13% lower peak memory, and 19% fewer parameters. Six formal theorems cover memory capacity, Lyapunov stability, gradient flow, expressivity separation, Pareto-optimal chunk size, and hybrid optimality.

</details>


### [155] [Bridging Talk and Thought: Understanding Dialogue Dynamics Across Collaborative Problem-Solving Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27233)
*Zhengyuan Liu,Stella Xin Yin,Min-Yen Kan,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出分层双层编码框架分析协作问题解决中的对话，整合认知与非认知机制及元认知调节，在九领域数据集验证有效性，揭示元认知调节是深度协作关键区分因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析框架无法充分捕捉人机/多智能体协作中的动态交互，随着智能系统自主性增强，亟需新框架优化协作评估与优化。

Method: 采用分层双层编码体系：底层整合认知与非认知问题解决维度，上层嵌入元认知调节机制，实现多维度对话分析。

Result: 跨九个领域数据集验证框架有效性，发现人类与智能体通过知识/技能协调解决复杂问题，元认知调节显著区分协作深度。

Conclusion: 该框架突破传统分析局限，为理解协作机制提供新范式，元认知调节成为衡量协作质量的核心指标。

Abstract: We present a conceptual framework for analyzing dialogue in collaborative problem-solving contexts, with an emphasis on the emerging dynamics of human-AI and multi-agent collaboration. As intelligent systems become active agents capable of autonomous reasoning and strategic cooperation, understanding the dialogic interaction during collaborative problem solving is increasingly important for optimizing and evaluating such partnerships. Our framework addresses key limitations in current analytical approaches through a hierarchical two-layer coding scheme that integrates cognitive and non-cognitive problem solving with metacognitive regulatory mechanisms. We demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability across nine datasets spanning multiple domains, and provide insights into how humans and agents coordinate their knowledge, skills, and efforts to solve complex problems, showing in particular that metacognitive regulation can be an essential discriminator of deeper collaboration.

</details>


### [156] [LMs as Task-Specific Knowledge Bases: An Interpretability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27237)
*Amit Elhelo,Amir Globerson,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型的知识存储是任务特定的，而非统一的知识库，影响其可靠性和可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证语言模型是否具备知识库的关键特性：对同一事实的不同查询返回一致结果，从而检验"参数即知识库"的假设。

Method: 通过行为分析（训练过程中知识涌现模式）和机制分析（参数定位实验）研究模型知识存储方式。

Result: 1. 同一事实的知识在不同任务中训练时难以共同涌现；2. 不同任务使用不同参数子集处理相同事实；3. 链式思维推理依赖任务特定参数。

Conclusion: 语言模型的知识与查询方式深度耦合于参数空间，推翻"知识库"类比，对知识可靠性与可控性提出挑战。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) capture large amounts of factual knowledge applicable to a wide range of tasks, motivating the view of their parameters as a knowledge base. An important property of knowledge bases is that different queries for the same fact return consistent results, drawing on a single source of truth. We investigate whether LMs satisfy this property through behavioral and mechanistic analyses. Our results suggest that they encode knowledge in a task-specific manner. Behaviorally, facts acquired on one task frequently fail to co-emerge on others during training. Parameter localization experiments suggest a mechanistic explanation, revealing distinct parameter subsets underlying different tasks for the same fact. Finally, we show that chain-of-thought reasoning draws part of its effectiveness from engaging task-specific parameters beyond those tied to the evaluation task. Our findings suggest that what the model knows and how it is asked are intertwined in parameter space, undermining the "knowledge base" analogy and carrying implications for the reliability and controllability of factual knowledge in LMs.

</details>


### [157] [How Surprising Is Historical Italian to Language Models? Tokenization Tax, Comprehension Tax, and a Simple Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27275)
*Maria Levchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出四维度诊断框架分析LLM处理历史语言的障碍，通过跨世纪数据集验证发现：历史文本虽增加编码成本但语义表示稳定，简单时间提示可显著降低生成不确定性，为数字图书馆安全部署LLM提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数字图书馆工作流中日益重要，但其处理历史语言的能力尚未被充分理解，传统研究将历史语言难度视为单一障碍，混淆了正字法变异、语言距离和预训练暴露等因素。

Method: 构建四维度诊断框架（分词成本、预测不确定性、语义鲁棒性、上下文敏感性），使用三个跨世纪数据集进行评估：17世纪意大利语语料库、19世纪意大利经典文本、18世纪俄语书籍。

Result: 发现编码成本与理解能力存在分离现象：俄语和早期意大利语分词成本相当（25-30%膨胀），但预测难度差异显著（17世纪意大利语 surprisal 为现代的2.4倍，学术文本达3.2倍，俄语增幅较小）；所有数据集嵌入相似度保持>0.85；添加时间上下文提示可降低60%的历史 surprisal。

Conclusion: 历史文本虽产生一致的编码税，但LLM的语义表示能力保持稳定，数字图书馆可安全用于语义检索任务，但生成应用需针对性适配。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly critical to digital library workflows, yet their ability to process historical language remains poorly understood. Historical difficulty is typically treated as a monolithic barrier, conflating orthographic variation, linguistic distance, and pretraining exposure. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic framework that decomposes this difficulty into four distinct dimensions: tokenization cost, predictive uncertainty (surprisal), semantic robustness, and context sensitivity.
  We evaluate this framework on three datasets spanning three centuries: (1) a newly curated corpus of 17th-century Italian texts (1610-1689) digitized from original page images; (2) canonical 19th-century Italian "I Promessi Sposi" serving as a high-exposure control; and (3) 18th-century Russian civil print books as a contrastive orthographic stress test.
  Our results reveal a distinct dissociation between encoding cost and comprehension. While Russian and early modern Italian incur comparable tokenization penalties (25-30% inflation), their predictive difficulty diverges sharply. 17th-century Italian is on average 2.4 times more surprising than its modern equivalent - with academic prose reaching 3.2 times - whereas Russian shows only a modest increase. But predictive uncertainty does not imply representational degradation: embedding similarity remains robust (> 0.85) across all datasets, confirming that models can represent historical meaning even when generation is unstable.
  Finally, we demonstrate that a minimal temporal context prompt reduces historical surprisal by approximately 60%, offering a simple, model-agnostic mitigation. These findings suggest that while historical text imposes a consistent encoding tax, digital libraries can safely deploy LLMs for semantic retrieval tasks, provided that generative applications are carefully adapted.

</details>


### [158] [Multilingual Reasoning Cascades Need More Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27306)
*Arnav Mazumder,Dengjia Zhang,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov,Niyati Bafna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出通过保留原始语言问题至翻译流程末端，构建上下文感知的翻译级联，有效缓解多语言推理中的信息损失问题，在285种语言的多语言基准测试中显著提升开放式生成任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统翻译级联方法（查询→英语→推理→目标语言）存在结构性信息损失，导致文化背景、语域及歧义消除线索丢失，影响多语言推理效果。

Method: 设计上下文感知的翻译级联架构：在最终翻译模块中同步输入原始问题、英语翻译问题及推理过程，无需额外训练。

Result: 在9个多语言基准（涵盖3种骨干模型、285种语言）中验证，开放式生成任务性能显著提升，且原始语言问题贡献了主要上下文信息增益。

Conclusion: 提出保留原始用户问题至流程末端的设计原则，为机器翻译级联的信息流优化提供可落地的解决方案，有效抑制错误传播。

Abstract: Translation cascades for reasoning translate the query from another language to English, reason in English, and translate the answer back to the original language. This is a competitive approach to multilingual reasoning, but structurally lossy, since each stage discards information later stages may need, including cues for cultural grounding, register, and disambiguation. We examine the benefits of a simple and training-free intervention: a context-aware translation cascade, which additionally provides the original question, the English translated question, and the reasoning trace to the context of the final translation module. We evaluate gains across nine multilingual benchmarks including various task types, three backbone models, and 285 high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, and demonstrate strong gains for open-ended generation across models and resource regimes. We show that the original language question carries most of the beneficial context. Our study emphasizes the need to better design information flow in machine translation cascades for mitigating error propagation, and provides a simple and actionable default strategy: preserve the original user question until the end of the pipeline.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Surface Forms: A Comprehensive, Mechanism-Oriented Taxonomy of Indirect Linguistic Encoding for LLM-Based Coded Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27314)
*Hamid Reza Firoozfar,Mohammadsadegh Abolhasani,Reza Mousavi,Paul Jen-Hwa Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向机制的间接语言表达（ILE）分类法，通过抽象底层编码/解码操作而非沟通目标，有效提升了LLM对社交媒体规避性语言的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户为规避内容审核而创造间接语言表达（如algospeak、委婉语等），现有分类法缺乏对编码机制的系统性解构，导致新兴编码语言检测能力不足。

Method: 构建机制导向的ILE分类体系，通过LLM提示词集成该分类法，在2000条人工标注的TikTok/Bluesky帖子上与4种现有分类法及无分类基线进行对比实验。

Result: 在三种LLM上均取得最优性能：文档级和片段级准确率较最佳基准提升4.7%，F1值提升5.4%，验证了分类法作为检测框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 机制导向分类法为动态演变的编码语言检测提供了稳定技术框架，可显著增强内容审核系统对规避性表达的识别能力。

Abstract: To avoid moderation and surveillance on social media, some users routinely invent indirect linguistic expressions (ILE) that camouflage sensitive meanings. Such expressions surface as algospeak, euphemisms, and adversarial obfuscation, depending on intent and context, and they involve recurring encoding mechanisms. We propose a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy of ILE that abstracts away from communicative goals and instead categorizes the underlying operations through which meaning is encoded and recovered. We evaluate the taxonomy by incorporating it into LLM prompts and comparing it with four existing taxonomies and a no-taxonomy baseline, using 2,000 manually annotated TikTok and Bluesky posts. The proposed taxonomy attains the strongest document- and span-level performance across the three LLMs, achieving an improvement of 4.7% in accuracy and 5.4% in F1 over the best-performing benchmark. The empirical results reveal the importance of a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy as a stable scaffold for detecting emerging coded language and a useful input to content moderation. Disclaimer: This paper contains content that may be profane, vulgar, or offensive.

</details>


### [160] [LLM-Based Examination of Eligibility Criteria from Securities Prospectuses at the German Central Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27316)
*Serhii Hamotskyi,Akash Kumar Gautam,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次将大语言模型（LLM）应用于德国央行证券抵押品资格验证任务，提出生成式信息提取框架，通过分解为提取、标准化和解释三阶段，结合基于价值的LLM评判方法，在文档级资格评估中实现91%高精确度，显著降低误接受风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于NER的手动验证方法面临OCR噪声、双语文本处理困难、依赖大量标注数据等问题，导致资源消耗高且灵活性不足，亟需更智能的自动化解决方案。

Method: 构建三阶段生成式信息提取流程：1) 使用LLM进行动态信息提取；2) 标准化处理多语言混杂内容；3) 基于语义规则解释结果。创新性引入LLM-as-a-judge评估体系，替代传统位置匹配指标。

Result: 系统在文档级资格判定中达到91%精确度，呈现保守决策特征（优先避免误接受），有效应对OCR噪声和德英双语文本挑战，验证了生成式方法在复杂金融文档处理中的优越性。

Conclusion: 本研究证实LLM可突破传统NER局限，为金融合规自动化提供新范式。提出的评估方法论具有普适性，为监管科技领域提供了可复现的技术路径。

Abstract: Verifying the eligibility of securities as collateral is a key responsibility of the German Central Bank. However, manually verifying these assets against legal and financial criteria within lengthy, semi-structured, and often bilingual prospectuses is a resource-intensive task. While previous efforts utilized traditional Named Entity Recognition (NER) for information extraction, these methods can struggle with OCR noise, linguistic variance, and rigid span-based constraints, and the need for manually annotated training data for each relevant annotation type. In this paper, we present the first case study applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to the eligibility examination process, shifting the paradigm toward a generative Information Extraction pipeline. Our approach decomposes the task into extraction, normalization, and interpretation, allowing for greater flexibility in handling noisy text and interleaved German-English content. We further introduce a value-based evaluation methodology using LLM-as-a-judge, which offers a more semantic assessment than location-based metrics. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based systems achieve high precision (up to 91%) in document-level eligibility, exhibiting a conservative operating profile that minimizes false acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27330)
*Tianyi Men,Zhuoran Jin,Pengfei Cao,Yubo Chen,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出PEEU方法解决小型多模态语言模型在web agent任务中规划能力弱和跨网站泛化差的问题，通过自主探索和经验利用生成高质量训练数据，7B模型达到30.6%准确率，超越更大的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态web agent需要有效任务规划来分解复杂GUI任务。小型开源MLLM虽具有成本效益和隐私保护优势，但存在规划能力弱和跨网站泛化能力有限的局限，需要改进其规划能力和泛化性能。

Method: 提出规划经验探索与利用(PEEU)方法，通过自主环境探索发现经验，利用事后经验合成严格对齐的高层训练数据。同时提出任务分解层次分析框架(TDHAF)，系统研究低层、中层、高层三个粒度的组合泛化行为。

Result: 在真实世界基准测试中，7B模型达到30.6%准确率，优于更大的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型。分析发现掌握低层原子技能不保证高层规划能力，而高层任务训练能产生更强的OOD泛化能力。

Conclusion: 构建事后高层任务和利用经验是提升小型多模态语言模型OOD规划能力的关键，证明了PEEU方法的有效性和小模型在web agent任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal web agents can assist humans in operating repetitive GUI tasks, where effective task planning is essential for decomposing complex tasks into executable actions. While small open source MLLMs are cost efficient and privacy preserving compared with commercial large models, they suffer from weak planning and limited cross website generalization. To address these limitations, we introduce the planning experience exploration and utilization (PEEU) method, which autonomously explores environments to discover experiences and utilizes hindsight experience to synthesize strictly aligned, high level training data. To quantitatively analyze the generalization behaviors driving this performance, we propose the task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework (TDHAF) to systematically study compositional generalization across three task granularities: low, middle and high levels. Our analysis reveals that mastering low level atomic skills does not guarantee high level planning competence, while high level task training yields stronger OOD generalization. Experiments on real world benchmarks demonstrate PEEU's superior effectiveness: our 7B model achieves 30.6% accuracy, outperforming the much larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model. These demonstrate constructing hindsight high level tasks and leveraging experiences is crucial for OOD planning abilities of small MLLMs.

</details>


### [162] [Mapping Political-Elite Networks in Europe with a Multilingual Joint Entity-Relation Extraction Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27347)
*Kirill Solovev,Jana Lasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种开放权重的多语言实体-关系联合提取管道，通过构建带符号的时间知识图谱，从海量新闻语料中自动识别政治精英间的复杂关系网络，解决了传统方法依赖手工编码和跨语言能力不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治精英网络分析面临复杂非正式关系难以规模化观测的挑战，现有自动化文本分析方法多局限于简单共现关系，且缺乏跨语言能力和可扩展实体解析能力。

Method: 采用模块化开放权重架构，结合基于跨度的命名实体识别与三阶段链接级联（映射至语言无关的Wikidata标识符），通过本体约束的混合专家模型进行引导解码，提取符合领域本体的有向符号化关系。

Result: 在3491条关系金标准上验证显示文本正确率达68.2%-93.7%；奥地利案例成功重构政党生命周期及人员流向，波兰案例揭示国家企业庇护网络与两党冲突网络的符号平衡结构。

Conclusion: 该框架通过桥接多语言原始文本与结构化关系数据，为跨国计算社会科学提供了可复制、可扩展的实证研究基础。

Abstract: Whether political elites organise into rent-seeking coalitions that capture public resources or civic networks that sustain governance is a central question in comparative politics. Yet observing these complex, informal, and adversarial ties at scale has historically required intensive manual coding, while automated text-as-data methods have largely been limited to simple co-occurrence. Recent large language model (LLM) approaches offer a path forward but often rely on proprietary APIs, lack cross-lingual capability, and struggle with scalable entity resolution. We present a modular, fully open-weight pipeline for multilingual joint entity-relation extraction that builds signed, temporal knowledge graphs from massive unstructured news corpora. It combines span-based named-entity recognition (NER) with a three-stage linking cascade mapping mentions to language-independent Wikidata identifiers; a high-throughput, ontology-constrained mixture-of-experts model then uses guided decoding to extract directed, signed relationships grounded in a domain ontology. A full-coverage spot-check against a 3491-relation gold standard shows high textual correctness (68.2% strict to 93.7% lenient). Two large-scale case studies validate the pipeline against the public record. In Austria, it reconstructs a political party's complete lifecycle, dating internal fractures and tracking personnel into successor factions and court convictions. In a Polish corpus, it uncovers the overlapping economic and governance networks of state-enterprise patronage, alongside the structurally balanced, signed conflict network of the polarized Civic Platform (Platforma Obywatelska, PO)--Law and Justice (Prawo i Sprawiedliwość, PiS) duopoly. By bridging raw multilingual text and structured relational data, our framework provides a robust, replicable foundation for cross-national empirical computational social science.

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>

# eess.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Tracking the Turn: Mamba-Powered Human Orientation Detection using UWB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26187)
*Mohammad Cheraghinia,Adnan Shahid,Jaron Fontaine,Cedric De Cock,David Plets,Eli De Poorter*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于UWB信道脉冲响应的纯UWB偏航方向预测方法，结合双向Mamba架构与卡尔曼滤波后处理，在多个场景中实现显著误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内定位系统多聚焦位置估计，身体方向依赖惯性测量单元等辅助设备，缺乏直接、可靠的方向感知能力，限制了上下文感知应用（如互动博物馆、智能门禁）的体验质量。

Method: 1. 使用固定锚点接收可穿戴标签的UWB信道脉冲响应（CIR）数据；2. 采用双向Mamba架构建模锚点间时序依赖；3. 引入身体部位条件模块适配标签佩戴位置差异；4. 双阶段卡尔曼滤波后处理：方向滤波平滑预测，位置滤波融合航向修正。

Result: 原始模型平均绝对误差38.6°（优于规则基线49.5°），经位置卡尔曼滤波后降至18.9°（误差减少51%），验证了跨场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法首次实现纯UWB高精度方向感知，通过深度时序建模与多源滤波协同优化，为无惯性传感器的室内人机交互提供新范式。

Abstract: User orientation is crucial for many context-aware applications, including interactive museum experiences, smart door access, and intuitive human-environment interaction. However, most existing indoor localization systems focus on estimating position, while body orientation is typically assigned to secondary devices such as inertial measurement units. In this paper, we propose a purely UWB-based approach that predicts yaw orientation directly from UWB Channel Impulse Response (CIR) measurements recorded at fixed anchors as they receive transmissions from a single wearable tag. We use a bidirectional Mamba architecture that captures dependencies across the anchor observations through forward and backward recurrent scans. The model uses per-anchor CIR and a body-part conditioning module to adapt the representation to different tag placements on the body. Two different Kalman filters are used as post-processing stages to exploit temporal continuity: an orientation-based filter that smooths the neural network predictions, and a location-based filter that additionally incorporates position-derived heading corrections. We evaluated the model's performance in different scenarios to ensure generalizability. The proposed Mamba model achieves a mean absolute error of 38.6 degrees in its raw form, outperforming a rule-based baseline of 49.5 degrees. With the location-based Kalman filter, the error is further reduced to 18.9 degrees, corresponding to a 51% reduction.

</details>


### [164] [Parametric Generalized Adaptive Moment Features (PG-AMF) for Bearing Fault Diagnosis and Machine Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26317)
*Rajeev Kumar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种参数自适应特征提取框架，结合多传感器融合机制，直接从振动信号中学习故障特征，在齿轮箱轴承数据集上验证了分类性能提升和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计特征方法依赖预设描述符，诊断灵敏度受限；深度学习虽具表征能力但需大量数据且可解释性差，亟需一种自适应、高可解释性的故障诊断方法。

Method: 构建参数自适应特征提取框架，提取绝对特征（能量分布）、符号矩特征（波形不对称性）、交流耦合矩特征（动态波动），并通过结构化融合机制建模多传感器通道交互。

Result: 在含五种健康状态的基准数据集上，分类性能优于传统方法，交叉验证结果一致；低维投影显示特征聚类更清晰，表明特征分离性显著增强。

Conclusion: 该方法有效捕捉多尺度信号特征，兼顾诊断性能与工业实用性，为旋转机械预测性维护提供了可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate fault diagnosis of rolling element bearings in rotating machinery is considered essential for ensuring industrial safety and enabling predictive maintenance. Conventional statistical feature-based methods rely on predefined descriptors, whose diagnostic sensitivity is constrained by fixed configurations and limited adaptability across varying fault conditions. Although deep learning approaches offer strong representational capacity, their effectiveness is often restricted by high data requirements and reduced interpretability. In this work, a parametric adaptive feature extraction framework is proposed, in which feature characteristics are learned directly from data rather than being manually specified. Multiple complementary representations are extracted from vibration signals, including absolute features capturing signal energy distribution, signed moment features reflecting waveform asymmetry, and AC-coupled moment features emphasizing dynamic fluctuations, while interactions between multiple sensor channels are modeled through a structured fusion mechanism to enhance fault representation. The proposed approach is evaluated on a benchmark gearbox bearing dataset comprising five health conditions, including normal operation and multiple fault types. Improved classification performance is observed compared to conventional methods, with consistent results under cross-validation, indicating strong generalization capability. Additionally, enhanced feature separability is demonstrated through clearer clustering patterns in low-dimensional projections. The learned representations effectively capture a wide range of signal characteristics, supporting both improved diagnostic performance and practical applicability in industrial monitoring systems.

</details>


### [165] [MIMO Zak-OTFS: Channel Estimation, Detection, and Throughput Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26420)
*Faraz Barati,Rahul Kumar Jaiswal,Saif Khan Mohammed,Ronny Hadani,Jeffrey G. Andrews*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究将Zak-OTFS调制扩展至MIMO框架，首次建立基于物理多径信道的完整系统模型，并提出DD域结构化导频放置的信道估计方法。实验表明Zak-OTFS在MIMO场景下仍优于CP-OFDM，尤其在抗多普勒频移和ICI方面；但存在SNR/多普勒交叉性能拐点，且对导频功率比更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zak-OTFS在SISO系统中已证明优于CP-OFDM，但其在MIMO系统中的性能潜力尚未充分探索，且缺乏直接基于物理信道的系统建模和高效信道估计方案。

Method: 1. 推导首个基于物理多径信道的MIMO Zak-OTFS完整系统模型；2. 设计DD域结构化导频放置方案实现高效信道估计。

Result: 在CDL-C信道模型下验证：1) Zak-OTFS的抗多普勒和ICI优势延续至MIMO系统；2) 发现SNR与多普勒存在性能交叉点（低SNR/低多普勒时CP-OFDM略优，高SNR/高多普勒时Zak-OTFS显著占优），且交叉点呈反向变化关系；3) MIMO Zak-OTFS对导频功率比(PDR)更敏感，但最优PDR与CP-OFDM相近。

Conclusion: MIMO Zak-OTFS在高速移动和高频场景具有显著优势，其性能交叉特性为实际部署提供关键指导，但需优化导频功率配置以平衡性能与资源开销。

Abstract: Zak-Orthogonal Time Frequency Space (Zak-OTFS) modulation has demonstrated substantial performance gains over cyclic-prefix orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (CP-OFDM) in highly time- and frequency-selective channels. In this paper, we extend Zak-OTFS to a multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) framework. We first derive a complete system model for MIMO Zak-OTFS based directly on the physical multipath channel; ours is the first work to do so. We then propose an efficient channel estimation method using structured pilot placement in the delay-Doppler (DD) domain. The proposed approach is evaluated under the standardized CDL-C channel model, demonstrating that the advantages of Zak-OTFS observed in SISO scenarios extend to MIMO systems, particularly its robustness to Doppler and inter-carrier interference (ICI). We identify a fundamental crossover behavior: CP-OFDM performs slightly better at low SNR and low Doppler, while Zak-OTFS excels at higher SNR or under severe Doppler dispersion. Furthermore, we show that the crossover points for SNR and Doppler shift inversely to each other. We also observe that Zak-OTFS, particularly with MIMO, exhibits increased sensitivity to high values of pilot-to-data power ratio (PDR), but has a similar optimal PDR as CP-OFDM.

</details>


### [166] [Multi-Modal Environment-Aware Beam Management for Massive MIMO: A Geometry-Driven Virtual Base Station Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26567)
*Yijie Bian,Wei Guo,Jie Yang,Shenghui Song,Jun Zhang,Shi Jin,Khaled B. Letaief*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于环境感知的波束管理框架，利用LiDAR点云构建虚拟基站数据库，结合几何建模与深度强化学习实现高效波束选择


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高频大规模MIMO系统中波束训练开销过大且多用户协调复杂，传统穷举式训练方法效率低下

Method: 1) 基于LiDAR点云构建VBS数据库；2) 通过镜像对称建模主反射路径；3) 开发粗粒度信道重建机制；4) 设计VOP部分波束训练方案；5) 提出分层双智能体DD3QN-CBS框架解决波束选择问题

Result: 仿真验证相比启发式和基线学习方法，在波束训练效率和波束选择性能上均有显著提升

Conclusion: 环境感知通信范式可有效降低波束管理开销，几何驱动框架与深度强化学习的结合为高频MIMO系统提供了可扩展解决方案

Abstract: High-frequency massive multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems promise ultra-high data rates. However, efficient beam management remains challenging due to the prohibitive beam training overhead and intricate coordination required in multi-user MIMO (MU-MIMO) scenarios. To address these bottlenecks, environment-aware communications have emerged as a promising paradigm, leveraging site-specific knowledge to circumvent exhaustive pilot-based beam training and streamline multi-user communications. In this paper, we propose an interpretable and geometry-driven framework that utilizes multi-modal environmental data, specifically regional 3D light detection and ranging (LiDAR) point clouds and location information, to construct an offline virtual base station (VBS) database. By modeling dominant reflection paths via mirror symmetry across building facades reconstructed from the point clouds, the VBS database provides a compact and sparse description of the wireless propagation environment. To bridge the semantic gap between geometric information and wireless channels, we develop a coarse channel reconstruction mechanism that estimates channel parameters directly from VBS-derived geometric relationships. Based on the resulting coarse beamspace representation, we design a VBS-assisted orthogonal-pilot (VOP)-based partial beam training scheme to refine the coarse estimates with minimal online training overhead. Finally, to tackle the combinatorial beam selection problem and manage inter-user interference, we propose a hierarchical deep reinforcement learning framework, namely a dual-agent dueling double deep Q-network, for coordinated beam selection (DD3QN-CBS). Simulation results demonstrate consistent gains in both beam training efficiency and beam selection performance over heuristic and learning-based baselines.

</details>


### [167] [State-Specific Respiratory Signatures for Affective and Stress Recognition: Interpretable Respiratory Markers, Autocorrelation Lags, and Compact CNN Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26723)
*Andrei Velichko,Mehmet Tahir Huyut*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究通过结合1D-CNN与手工设计的呼吸特征，分析呼吸数据在压力及情感状态识别中的表现。发现CNN在压力检测中表现最佳，而手工特征在基线、娱乐和冥想等状态中提供更强的生理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注分类准确率，却忽视识别区分不同状态的呼吸特征。本研究将呼吸信号识别重新框架为联合预测与解释问题，旨在揭示状态特异性呼吸标记。

Method: 使用WESAD数据集胸部呼吸通道，采用留一法验证60秒窗口数据。结合两种方法：1D-CNN处理原始信号，以及物理分组的手工呼吸特征（包括时序、变异性、波形统计、频谱/时频、自相关/非线性预测性等）。引入自相关过渡滞后（Zpm/Zmp）作为可解释标记，并评估FEG-Pro/Lyapunov类描述符。

Result: CNN在压力vs非压力检测中表现最佳（准确率96.72%，macro-F1 95.30%，MCC 90.61%）。手工特征在基线（MCC 65.34%）、娱乐（MCC 35.69%）和冥想（MCC 88.65%）状态中更有效，尤其冥想状态表现突出。

Conclusion: CNN适用于实际压力检测任务，而手工设计的呼吸特征为多种非压力状态提供更强的生理透明性标记，揭示不同状态下的特异性呼吸模式。

Abstract: Respiratory activity is a direct and interpretable physiological channel for wearable stress and affective-state recognition, yet many studies emphasize classification accuracy without identifying which respiratory properties separate different states. This work reframes RESP-based recognition as a joint predictive and explanatory problem. Using the chest respiratory channel of the WESAD dataset, we analyze 60 s windows under leave-one-subject-out validation and combine two complementary branches: compact raw-signal one-dimensional convolutional neural networks (1D-CNNs) and physically grouped handcrafted respiratory signatures. The primary application task is binary stress versus non-stress detection, while baseline, stress, amusement, and meditation are additionally analyzed in a one-vs-rest setting to reveal state-specific respiratory markers. The feature space is organized into respiratory timing, breath-to-breath variability, waveform statistics, spectral/time-frequency descriptors, and autocorrelation/nonlinear predictability descriptors, with the raw 60 s signal treated as a sixth representation for the CNN branch. We introduce autocorrelation transition lags (Zpm/Zmp) as interpretable markers of respiratory correlation scale and separately evaluate exploratory FEG-Pro/Lyapunov-like descriptors. In the final CNN refit setting, the raw-signal model achieved the strongest stress-vs-rest performance, with accuracy 96.72 percent, macro-F1 95.30 percent, and MCC 90.61 percent. In contrast, compact feature models were stronger for baseline, with MCC 65.34 percent, amusement, with MCC 35.69 percent, and especially meditation, with MCC 88.65 percent. These results show that CNNs are most useful for the practical stress detector, whereas interpretable respiratory signatures provide stronger and more physiologically transparent state-specific markers for several non-stress conditions.

</details>


### [168] [Collision-resistant multi-channel M-ASPM configurations with shared single detection channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26896)
*Alexei V. Nikitin,Ruslan L. Davidchack*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: M-ASPM调制技术通过分离检测通道与有效载荷处理，在提升接收灵敏度的同时避免碰撞率上升，实现LPWAN网络的可扩展性与经济性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LPWAN调制在延长符号持续时间以提升接收灵敏度时，会显著增加空中时间（ToA），导致碰撞暴露加剧。M-ASPM旨在解决这一矛盾，同时支持多通道共享以优化部署成本。

Method: 利用M-ASPM短前导码作为独立碰撞抗性检测通道，实现异步包检测、同步及载波频率偏移（CFO）估计；通过动态调整处理增益，在不扩展符号采样窗口的情况下提取有效载荷，并构建多通道共享架构。

Result: 仿真验证表明，在高碰撞率、多变有效载荷及处理增益条件下，M-ASPM显著降低噪声/干扰影响，保持吞吐量稳定，且支持准正交通道共享机制。

Conclusion: M-ASPM通过结构创新解耦了范围扩展与碰撞导致的吞吐量下降问题，为LPWAN提供了可扩展、低碰撞的调制方案，具有显著工程应用价值。

Abstract: M-ary Aggregate Spread Pulse Modulation (M-ASPM) is a physical layer (PHY) modulation technique that offers several advantages for low-power wide-area networks (LPWANs). For instance, in conventional LPWAN modulations increasing receiver sensitivity by extending symbol duration - thereby proportionally increasing the time-on-air (ToA) - exacerbates collision exposure. In contrast, M-ASPM payload processing gain can vary over a wide range without impacting the effective packet collision rate. In particular, in this work we demonstrate how short front portions of M-ASPM packets can serve as a separate collision-resistant detection channel that, in addition to performing asynchronous packet detection and synchronization, obtains the carrier frequency offset (CFO) for each packet within a desired range and with the required precision. Then, while raising processing gain, the subsequent payload information can be extracted without expanding the sample window per symbol. Consequently, the receiver sensitivity can be significantly increased without exacerbating packet collisions and thus without reducing network throughput under collision-limited operation. We further establish a multi-channel configuration in which numerous quasi-orthogonal payload channels share a single detection channel that additionally performs payload channel identification and selection. Such sharing is especially useful for scaling and economizing LPWAN deployments under diverse technical requirements and constraints. The presented analysis is validated via extensive simulations under high packet collision rates in wide ranges of payload sizes and processing gains, and for varying noise and interference power levels. The results signify that M-ASPM provides a structurally distinct scaling behavior compared to conventional LPWAN modulations, decoupling range extension from collision-induced throughput degradation.

</details>


### [169] [Distributed Massive MIMO with 1-Bit Radio-over-Fiber Fronthaul: Uplink Spectral Efficiency and Power Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26939)
*Alireza Bordbar,Anzhong Hu,Giuseppe Durisi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: This paper addresses uplink spectral efficiency in D-MIMO systems with 1-bit radio-over-fiber fronthaul, proposing novel achievable-rate expressions using Bussgang decomposition to handle quantization-induced nonlinearity and lack of MMSE channel estimates. Optimal signal-to-dither ratio (SDR) is determined for single/multiuser scenarios, and max-min fairness under dynamic range constraints is analyzed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The D-MIMO architecture with 1-bit fronthaul eliminates local oscillators for coherent transmission but introduces challenges: nonlinear input-output relations due to quantization and unavailability of MMSE channel estimates, rendering conventional spectral efficiency methods inapplicable.

Method: Novel achievable-rate expressions leveraging Bussgang decomposition to linearize the quantized signal model, avoiding reliance on MMSE estimates. These expressions are used to optimize SDR and evaluate oversampling effects in single/multiuser setups, along with analyzing max-min fairness under dynamic range limitations.

Result: Determination of optimal SDR values maximizing achievable rates, quantification of oversampling impact, and characterization of fairness performance when access points cannot maintain ideal SDR due to hardware constraints.

Conclusion: The proposed framework enables accurate spectral efficiency analysis and optimization in quantized D-MIMO systems, offering practical solutions for hardware-constrained deployments while maintaining system performance through SDR tuning and fairness-aware design.

Abstract: We analyze the uplink spectral efficiency achievable in a distributed multiple-input multiple-output (D-MIMO) architecture employing a 1-bit radio-over-fiber fronthaul. This architecture eliminates the need for local oscillators at the access points, hence enabling coherent-phase transmission without costly over-the-air synchronization. With this fronthaul architecture, the uplink signal at the central processing unit is a dithered, oversampled, and 1-bit quantized version of the passband signal received at the access points. This makes some of the conventional spectral-efficiency expressions used in the D-MIMO literature not directly applicable for two key reasons: the nonlinearity of the input-output relation and the practical unavailability of minimum mean square error (MMSE) channel estimates. To address this issue, we propose novel achievable-rate expressions that do not require MMSE channel estimates and rely on the Bussgang decomposition to linearize the input-output relation. We use these expressions to determine the optimal signal-to-dither ratio (SDR) that maximizes the achievable rates in both single- and multiuser scenarios and to assess the impact of oversampling. We then use one of the proposed achievable-rate expressions to investigate the max-min fairness problem when the access points cannot maintain the optimal SDR because of limitations in their dynamic range.

</details>


### [170] [Angle Estimation via WFRFT Spatial-Domain Basis Decomposition: Breaking the Rayleigh Resolution Limit with Structured Waveform Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26957)
*Wang Hao,Zhang Kuang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四分量加权型分数傅里叶变换(4-WFRFT)的MIMO雷达角度估计框架，通过空间域波形分集机制实现超越瑞利衍射极限的角度分辨率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MIMO雷达中的分数傅里叶变换(FrFT)仅作为接收端时频处理工具，无法充分利用波形分集优势。本文旨在通过空间域波形分集机制，突破传统瑞利衍射极限，提升低信噪比环境下的角度估计性能。

Method: 将数据序列分解为四个WFRFT基函数(原始信号、傅里叶变换、时间反转、逆傅里叶变换)，从四元均匀线性阵列同时传输。通过远场角度处的空间叠加创造独特的角度相关波形结构，利用时域匹配滤波进行角度估计。

Result: 实现了1.4×到12.8×的瑞利衍射极限超越，在低信噪比下优势尤为显著。Fisher信息分析显示WFRFT波形结构贡献约3×于阵列几何的信息量。扩展到M元阵列时分辨率增益随阵列规模扩展。线性调频基序列仿真验证了0dB PAPR和亚瑞利分辨率。

Conclusion: 该空间域WFRFT波形分集机制为MIMO雷达角度估计提供了新的技术路径，在突破传统分辨率极限的同时保持了良好的旁瓣特性，为高精度雷达感知系统提供了理论基础和实践指导。

Abstract: We propose a MIMO radar angle estimation framework that uses the four-component weighted-type fractional Fourier transform (4-WFRFT) as a spatial-domain waveform diversity mechanism. Unlike conventional fractional Fourier (FrFT) MIMO radar where FrFT serves as a receiver-side time-frequency processing tool, our approach decomposes a data sequence into four WFRFT basis functions,original signal, its Fourier transform, time-reversal, and inverse Fourier transform, and transmits them simultaneously from a four-element uniform linear array. The spatial superposition of these basis functions at each far-field angle creates a unique angle-dependent waveform structure, enabling angle estimation through time-domain matched filtering with known waveforms. We demonstrate that this spatial-domain mixing achieves angular resolution surpassing the Rayleigh diffraction limit by a factor of 1.4$\times$ to 12.8$\times$, with the advantage most pronounced at low SNR where conventional beamforming fails completely. The Cramér-Rao bound is derived with a full 3-parameter Fisher information matrix, and the Fisher information is decomposed into geometry and waveform contributions, revealing that the WFRFT waveform structure contributes approximately 3$\times$ more information than array geometry alone. Extension to $M$-element arrays with $M$-component WFRFT demonstrates resolution gain scaling with array size. Simulations with linear chirp base sequences achieve 0\,dB PAPR and validate sub-Rayleigh resolution with a four-element array.

</details>


### [171] [Inverse Design of Compact and Wideband Inverted Doherty Power Amplifiers Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27002)
*Han Zhou,Haojie Chang,David Widen,Christian Fager*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种深度学习辅助的逆合成方法，用于设计紧凑宽带倒置Doherty功率放大器，通过CNN和遗传算法联合优化生成像素化合成网络，在1.9-2.5GHz频段实现了51%-63%的峰值效率和48%-54%的回退效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Doherty功率放大器设计复杂，难以同时实现宽带、高效率和小尺寸。需要一种新的设计方法来整合负载调制、阻抗匹配、功率合成和相位补偿等多功能，并提高设计效率和性能。

Method: 采用深度学习辅助的逆合成方法，联合使用卷积神经网络(CNNs)和遗传算法(GAs)来生成像素化Doherty合成器网络，将所有关键功能整合到单一结构中。使用GaN HEMT技术设计和制造原型验证。

Result: 原型在1.9-2.5GHz频段实现了峰值漏极效率51%-63%，6dB回退效率48%-54%，输出功率44±0.3dBm。应用数字预失真后，相邻信道泄漏比优于-53.2dBc，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 深度学习辅助的逆合成方法为宽带Doherty功率放大器设计提供了新的解决方案，能够高效地生成集成多功能的像素化合成网络，在GaN HEMT平台上验证了其优越的性能表现。

Abstract: This paper presents a deep learning-assisted methodology for the inverse synthesis of a compact, wideband inverted Doherty power amplifier (PA). Convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and genetic algorithms (GAs) are jointly employed to generate pixelated Doherty combiner networks that integrate load modulation, impedance matching, power combining, and phase compensation into a single structure. As a proof of concept, we design and fabricate a GaN HEMT Doherty PA with a pixelated output combiner. The prototype achieves a measured peak drain efficiency of 51%-63% and a 6-dB back-off efficiency of 48%-54% over 1.9-2.5 GHz. Within the same frequency range, the measured output power is 44+/-0.3 dBm. Furthermore, with digital predistortion (DPD) applied, the prototype circuit demonstrates an adjacent channel leakage ratio (ACLR) better than -53.2 dBc.

</details>


### [172] [Low Complexity Kolmogorov-Arnold Network-based DPD for Analog RoF Fronthaul](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27042)
*Carlos Daniel Fontes da Silva,Tianyu Jiang,Lu Zhang,Vjaceslavs Bobrovs,Xianbin Yu,Xiaodan Pang,Oskars Ozolins,Edson Porto da Silva*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 首次提出基于Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）的数字预失真模型ETDKAN，通过符号化实现低计算复杂度与高可解释性，在A-RoF系统中实现4-5dB ACLR改善，性能媲美神经网络模型但复杂度接近传统记忆多项式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决模拟光纤传输系统(A-RoF)中射频非线性器件导致的信号失真问题，突破现有神经网络DPD模型计算复杂度高、可解释性差的技术瓶颈，同时保持记忆多项式方法的低复杂度优势。

Method: 构建融合射频器件物理约束的ETDKAN架构，采用KAN符号化技术将非线性映射转化为可解析的数学表达式，与MLP及记忆多项式DPD进行联合优化训练。

Result: 符号化ETDKAN(symbETDKAN)在ACLR和EVM指标上达到神经网络级性能，计算复杂度仅略高于记忆多项式；实验验证显示在典型A-RoF场景中实现4-5dB ACLR改善。

Conclusion: ETDKAN成功平衡了性能与复杂度，其符号化特性显著增强模型可解释性，实验证明该方法具备工程实用价值，为下一代A-RoF系统非线性补偿提供了新范式。

Abstract: This paper proposes and demonstrates experimentally for the first time a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN)-based digital predistortion (DPD) model, named envelope time-delay KAN (ETDKAN), for mitigating nonlinear distortions in analog radio-over-fiber (A-RoF) systems. The ETDKAN model incorporates physical constraints of radio-frequency (RF) nonlinear devices and, through KAN symbolization, achieves a significant reduction in computational complexity while improving interpretability. The proposed model is numerically implemented and optimized alongside multilayer perceptron (MLP) and memory-polynomial-based DPDs. Results show that the resulting symbolic ETDKAN (symbETDKAN) attains ACLR and EVM performance comparable to neural network-based models, while maintaining a computational complexity close to that of memory polynomials. Experimental validation using an A-RoF system confirms the practical feasibility of the proposed approach, which resulted in a 4-5 dB reduction in ACLR in the analyzed scenario.

</details>


### [173] [Single-Base-Station Indoor Localization via Super-Resolved Relative Power Delay Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27157)
*Fangqing Xiao,Dirk T. M. Slock*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于期望最大化稀疏贝叶斯学习（EM-SBL）的室内定位方法，通过建模噪声导频样本导致的狄利克雷模糊、延迟折叠和离网格不匹配问题，在QuaDRiGa仿真中实现93.27%的Top-1准确率和0.392米平均误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统室内定位依赖理想功率延迟谱（PDP）作为位置指纹，但实际通信接收机仅能获取有限噪声导频样本，导致PDP重建面临模糊、折叠和网格不匹配等挑战，限制了定位精度。

Method: 采用期望最大化稀疏贝叶斯学习（EM-SBL）算法，建立噪声导频样本到后验功率谱的映射模型，通过迭代优化解决Dirichlet模糊、延迟折叠和离网格不匹配问题。

Result: 在QuaDRiGa空间一致性仿真中，双倍SBL处理将20-dB Top-1准确率从原生PDP的75.79%和三次零填充的87.24%提升至93.27%，定位误差降至0.392米。

Conclusion: EM-SBL方法有效克服了实际信道观测的局限性，在复杂室内环境中显著提升基于PDP指纹的定位精度，验证了稀疏贝叶斯学习在非理想条件下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Indoor multipath is shaped by surrounding reflectors, scatterers, and blockages, so a relative power-delay profile (PDP) can serve as a location fingerprint without an identifiable LoS path, angle information, or absolute time-of-arrival ranging. However, a communication receiver observes finitely many noisy pilot-frequency samples rather than an ideal PDP. This paper models the resulting Dirichlet blur, delay folding, and off-grid mismatch, and reconstructs a posterior-power profile using expectation-maximization sparse Bayesian learning. In spatially consistent QuaDRiGa simulations, twofold SBL raises 20-dB Top-1 accuracy from 75.79\% (native PDP) and 87.24\% (threefold zero-padding) to 93.27\%, with 0.392~m mean error.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Machine Learning-Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization in D2D Clustered Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26119)
*Maede Rezaei,Ahmadreza Montazerolghaem*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于机器学习的内容流行度预测与缓存优化框架（ML CPCO），通过动态预测用户和集群级内容需求并考虑用户缓存参与意愿，优化D2D集群网络中的缓存放置策略，显著提升缓存利用率（达97%）和命中率，降低回传流量并改善用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信技术发展导致智能设备激增，高质量内容需求增长加剧蜂窝网络回传链路压力，引发拥堵和延迟。现有缓存方案未充分考虑用户异质性（如80%流量由20%用户产生），假设偏好均匀分布，导致缓存效率不足。

Method: 设计ML CPCO框架，结合机器学习算法预测未来内容请求和集群级流行度，整合用户缓存参与意愿，通过优化模型动态调整缓存放置策略，实现D2D集群网络中的高效内容分发。

Result: 仿真验证表明：该方案在不同网络条件下缓存利用率接近97%（对比方法最高），命中率显著提升，执行时间可控，有效减少回传流量并改善用户体验。

Conclusion: ML CPCO框架通过融合机器学习预测与用户异质性建模，突破了传统缓存策略的局限性，为边缘缓存优化提供了高效解决方案，对缓解网络拥堵和提升服务质量具有重要实践价值。

Abstract: Advancements in wireless communication technology have led to the widespread use of smart devices including computers, mobile phones, tablets, wearable devices, and vehicles which has significantly increased the demand for high quality content. This growing demand puts pressure on the backhaul links in cellular networks, resulting in congestion and content delivery delays. To address this, cache enabled networks and edge caching, such as caching in user devices, have emerged as promising solutions to reduce backhaul traffic. By caching content locally and using device to device (D2D) communication for retrieval, content delivery can be made more efficient. However, limited cache capacity requires intelligent content selection strategies. The popularity of the content is dynamic and varies with user preferences, where less than 20% of the users generate 80% of multimedia traffic. Many existing methods fail to consider this user heterogeneity, often assuming uniform preferences throughout the network. This paper proposes a novel Machine Learning Driven Content Popularity Prediction and Cache Optimization (ML CPCO) framework that dynamically predicts user and cluster level content demand, incorporates user willingness to participate in caching, and optimizes cache placement in D2D enabled clustered networks. The system predicts future content requests using machine learning algorithms and estimates content popularity at the cluster level. Based on these predictions, cache placement decisions are made to maximize efficiency. The simulation results show that the proposed approach performs well under various network conditions, achieving a cache utilization rate of nearly 97% the highest among the methods compared. In addition, it offers an improved hit rate with an acceptable execution time, resulting in reduced backhaul traffic and enhanced user experience.

</details>


### [175] [Dot-Flik: A Scalable Edge AI Architecture for Distributed Insect Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26121)
*Mattia Consani,Denisa-Andreea Constantinescu,Åse Håtveit,Titus Venverloo,Fabio Duarte,Carlo Ratti,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对昆虫监测系统的硬件成本与能耗问题，提出边缘运动帧过滤算法、分布式IoT架构及实际部署验证，实现高效低成本城市生物多样性监测网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉昆虫监测系统受限于高硬件成本、高能耗及依赖集中处理/云端连接，难以规模化部署。

Method: 1. 基于时间差分、伽马校正运动放大和块运动密度分析的运动信息帧过滤算法，边缘端丢弃无关帧；2. 分布式分层IoT架构，边缘预处理解耦数据采集与AI分类；3. 低成本硬件户外部署验证实时性、可扩展性、成本及能耗。

Result: 轻风条件下帧减少60-80%，实时30 FPS运行（计算余量12.8ms），节能22.6%，单节点支持5-6路并发边缘流。

Conclusion: 为城市环境高密度低成本生物多样性监测网络提供实用技术基础，显著提升覆盖范围与能效。

Abstract: Global insect population declines necessitate scalable, continuous monitoring systems, yet existing vision-based solutions remain constrained by high hardware costs, energy demands, and reliance on centralized processing or cloud connectivity. This article presents three contributions to address these limitations. First, we propose a motion-informed frame filtering algorithm based on temporal differencing, gamma-corrected motion amplification, and block-based motion density analysis that discards irrelevant frames at the edge while preserving insect activity, without requiring deep learning inference on the sensing device. Second, we introduce a distributed, hierarchical IoT architecture that decouples data acquisition from AI classification through this edge-level preprocessing, projecting fractional scaling of central processing requirements and significantly increasing monitoring coverage compared to monolithic single-stream approaches. Third, we validate the complete system through real-world outdoor deployments on low-cost commodity hardware along four axes: real-time performance, network scalability, hardware cost, and energy efficiency under varying wind conditions. Results demonstrate 60-80% frame reduction under light-wind conditions, sustained real-time 30 FPS operation with 12.8 ms of computational headroom, up to 22.6% energy savings, and support for 5-6 concurrent edge streams per central node. These findings establish a practical foundation for dense, low-cost biodiversity monitoring networks in urban environments.

</details>


### [176] [Privacy-Aware Agent Collaboration for Dynamic VR Slice Management in 6G SD-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26123)
*Khaled M. Naguib,Soumaya Cherkaoui,Mahmoud M. Elmesalawy,Ahmed M. Abd El-Haleem,Ibrahim I. Ibrahim*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种面向6G网络VR服务的多智能体强化学习框架，通过移动性预测和隐私保护机制优化资源分配，在仿真中实现了显著的性能提升和隐私安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络中VR服务对超低延迟和高吞吐量的需求给SD-RAN动态资源管理带来关键挑战，需要平衡资源效率与用户隐私保护。

Method: 设计移动性驱动、隐私感知的多智能体强化学习(MARL)框架，集成移动性预测模块和信息瓶颈编码器，实现端到端VR链路的协同资源分配。

Result: 仿真对比显示：相比传统方法，吞吐量提升34%，资源消耗降低28%，隐私泄露减少85%。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了6G环境下VR切片管理的核心矛盾，在保证沉浸式体验的同时实现了资源效率与隐私安全的协同优化。

Abstract: Ultra-low latency and high throughput are required for Virtual Reality (VR) services in 6G networks, which presents critical challenges for Software-Defined Radio Access Networks (SD-RANs) dynamic resource management. This work propose a mobility-driven, privacy-aware Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) framework for VR slice management, in which cooperative agents maximize resource distribution over end-to-end VR links while protecting the privacy of user data. Our approach incorporates mobility prediction and an information bottleneck encoder to facilitate effective and secure agent collaboration. In simulations, comparisons with traditional methods are studied which show up to 34\% throughput improvement, 28\% fewer resources, and 85\% less privacy leakage, guaranteeing dependable immersive VR experiences in future 6G environments.

</details>


### [177] [Enhancing FANET Routing Resilience: A Fuzzy-Driven Bio-Inspired Approach and Its Quantitative Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26124)
*Xinwang Yuan,Jinshu Su,Yusheng Xia,Congxi Song*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该研究针对无人机自组网中高动态环境下的聚类稳定性问题，提出多因素优化簇头选举算法、双层信标机制及自适应hello间隔控制策略，并通过参数敏感性指数评估路由韧性，在NS-3模拟中验证了性能优势但存在峰值性能与参数稳定性的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机自组网因高移动性导致拓扑快速变化和链路不稳定，传统聚类方法难以维持稳定结构，且现有控制消息频率调整策略存在开销与实时性的矛盾，缺乏量化评估路由韧性的有效指标。

Method: 1. 多因素优化改进人工蜂群簇头选举算法；2. 设计双层信标机制增强聚类稳定性；3. 提出基于模糊逻辑的hello间隔自适应控制器；4. 构建参数敏感性指数评估框架。

Result: NS-3模拟显示：所提协议在整体性能上显著优于基线方案，但峰值性能提升伴随参数稳定性下降，揭示了性能优化与鲁棒性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 通过多技术融合有效提升了动态网络中的聚类稳定性与传输效率，同时建立了可量化的路由韧性评估体系，为高动态场景下的网络协议设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: In flying ad hoc networks, the high mobility of unmanned aerial vehicles leads to rapid topology changes and unstable links. Clustering can simplify topology management, yet maintaining stable cluster structures remains challenging in highly dynamic environments. This paper employs multi-factor optimization to enhance the artificial bee colony-based cluster head election algorithm and introduces a dual-layer beacon mechanism for cluster maintenance, thereby improving cluster stability and reducing reconfiguration overhead. Meanwhile, although shortening the hello interval helps capture link variations in a timely manner, it significantly increases control overhead. To address this issue, a fuzzy logic-based hello interval controller is proposed to adaptively adjust control message frequency according to network dynamics, effectively reducing overhead while preserving transmission performance. Furthermore, to quantitatively evaluate routing resilience in large-scale and highly dynamic scenarios, this paper proposes a parameter sensitivity index to characterize both overall protocol performance and its sensitivity to environmental variations. The proposed protocol is compared with common baseline schemes in NS-3 simulations, and the resilience evaluation framework analysis reveals significant advantages in overall performance while exposing the trade-off between peak performance and parametric stability.

</details>


### [178] [Geometric Fairness-Aware Routing for Federated Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26125)
*Ratun Rahman*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Geo-FairFed是一种结合双曲图神经网络与联邦优化的路由系统，通过负曲率流形建模网络拓扑，在6G边缘场景中实现延迟、能耗与公平性的协同优化，实验表明其相比现有方法显著降低延迟20%、能耗17%，并提升公平性21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦路由系统过度关注聚合性能指标（如延迟/吞吐量），忽视网络拓扑的几何结构特性及边缘节点间的公平性需求，难以满足6G和边缘智能网络对均衡性能的要求。

Method: 提出Geo-FairFed框架：1) 利用双曲图神经网络在负曲率流形学习拓扑感知表示，捕捉层次关系与连接不对称性；2) 设计曲率正则化全局聚合器，联合优化路由损失、几何一致性与基于Jain公平指数的不平等惩罚项。

Result: 理论证明在有限曲率条件下收敛，并实现帕累托改进均衡；动态6G边缘和IoT拓扑仿真显示：平均延迟降低20%，能耗减少17%，公平性提升最高21%。

Conclusion: 将网络拓扑嵌入双曲流形并融合公平性约束至联邦更新机制，可显著提升大规模网络路由的效率与公平性，为下一代边缘智能网络提供新范式。

Abstract: Emerging 6G and edge-intelligent networks require effective and balanced routing algorithms among varied and spatially distributed devices. Existing federated routing systems often prioritize aggregate latency or throughput above fairness and the underlying geometric structure of network topologies. This paper describes Geo-FairFed, a geometric fairness-aware routing system that blends hyperbolic graph neural networks (HGNNs) and federated optimization to provide equal performance across edge nodes. Each node learns topology-aware representations on a negatively curved manifold, which include hierarchical relationships and connection asymmetries. A global aggregator next enforces fairness using a curvature-regularized aim that minimizes routing loss, geometric inconsistency, and an inequality penalty based on Jain's fairness index. A theoretical analysis develops convergence guarantees under limited curvature and shows that the proposed fairness term results in a Pareto-improving equilibrium in routing performance. Extensive simulations on dynamic 6G-edge and IoT topologies reveal that Geo-FairFed minimizes average latency by 20\%, reduces energy consumption by 17\%, and improves fairness by up to 21\% when compared to state-of-the-art federated and geometric routing protocols. The study found that embedding topology in a hyperbolic manifold and including fairness into federated updates can significantly enhance the efficiency and equity of large-scale network routing.

</details>


### [179] [HALO: Hierarchical Auction-assisted Learning for Offloading in SAGIN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26293)
*Xuli Cai,Poonam Lohan,Sachin Ravikant Trankatwar,Burak Kantarci*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究三层空天地一体化网络中的延迟感知任务卸载与资源调度，提出HALO分层拍卖辅助学习框架，结合拍卖机制与分层PPO算法，在不同流量负载下任务成功率显著优于传统DRL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对空天地一体化网络中IoT设备、UAV边缘节点和HAPS组成的三层架构，解决延迟敏感任务在动态环境下的资源分配和任务卸载问题，需要处理NP难的混合整数非线性规划问题。

Method: 提出宏微时隙模型跟踪系统动态，设计HALO框架：采用拍卖机制进行任务关联，使用分层近端策略优化(HPPO)进行带宽、发射功率和CPU频率的连续资源控制。

Result: 在不同流量负载下，HALO持续优于DRL基线：相比PPO任务成功率提高8.7个百分点(相对增益11.4%)，相比DDPG和SAC相对改进分别为32.4%和89.9%，展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HALO框架能够在变化的流量条件下保持稳定和高效性能，适合延迟敏感的SAGIN环境，为复杂网络资源调度提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate delay-aware task offloading and resource scheduling in a three-tier space-air-ground integrated network (SAGIN) consisting of IoT devices, UAV edge nodes, and a high-altitude platform station (HAPS). We formulate joint task association and continuous resource control (including bandwidth, transmit power, and CPU frequency allocation) as a non-convex mixed-integer nonlinear programming (MINLP) problem, which is inherently NP-hard. To capture fine-grained system dynamics, we introduce a macro-micro slot model that tracks cumulative transmission and computation progress over time. Based on this model, we propose HALO, a hierarchical auction-assisted learning framework that combines auction-based task association with hierarchical Proximal Policy Optimization (HPPO) for resource allocation. Simulation results under different traffic loads show that HALO consistently outperforms representative deep reinforcement learning (DRL) baselines. In particular, HALO achieves an average improvement of 8.7 percentage points in task success rate over PPO (corresponding to an 11.4% relative gain) and shows consistently greater robustness than DDPG and SAC, with relative improvements of 32.4% and 89.9%, respectively. These results highlight HALO's ability to maintain stable and efficient performance under varying traffic conditions, making it well-suited for delay-sensitive SAGIN environments.

</details>


### [180] [5G NR-V2X Scheduling Approaches for CPM Variable Size Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26746)
*Vittorio Todisco,Mattia Andreani,Maria Luisa Merani,Alessandro Bazzi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: CPM流量下的NR-V2X调度策略对比研究表明：资源使用稳定性比瞬时负载优化更重要，SPS+填充方案性能稳健，而动态调度易引发碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPM因动态对象包含和周期性安全开销导致数据包大小显著波动，而现有5G NR-V2X Mode 2的半持续调度（SPS）主要针对稳定大小的周期性流量设计，真实CPM流量对分布式资源分配的影响尚不明确。

Method: 基于真实感知数据集重建的CPM流量，进行系统级评估，对比动态调度及三种SPS方案：填充分配、激进尺寸驱动重选、MCS自适应。

Result: 数据包大小波动会显著降低可靠性；动态调度和激进重选因频繁资源重分配增加碰撞概率；SPS+填充实现稳定资源分配且性能稳健；MCS自适应平均接收比最高但可靠性分布不均。

Conclusion: 在真实CPM流量下，资源使用稳定性比瞬时负载优化更关键，为NR-V2X Mode 2上的CPS部署提供设计指导原则。

Abstract: Collective perception messages (CPMs) introduce significant packet size variability due to dynamic object inclusion and periodic security overhead. While 5G NR-V2X Mode 2 typically employs semi-persistent scheduling (SPS) designed for periodic traffic with relatively stable packet sizes, the impact of realistic CPM-driven size fluctuations on distributed resource allocation remains insufficiently understood. This paper presents a comparative system-level evaluation of NR-V2X Mode 2 scheduling strategies under variable-size CPM traffic reconstructed from real-world perception datasets. We analyze dynamic scheduling and multiple SPS-based approaches, including padding-based allocation, aggressive size-driven reselection, and modulation and coding scheme (MCS) adaptation. Results show that packet size variability can significantly degrade reliability when scheduling stability is compromised. In particular, dynamic scheduling and aggressive reselection increase collision probability due to frequent resource reallocations. In contrast, SPS with padding converges to a stable resource allocation and provides robust performance, while MCS adaptation achieves the highest average packet reception ratio but with uneven reliability across packet types. The findings demonstrate that, under realistic CPM traffic, stability of resource usage is more critical than instantaneous load optimization, and provide design guidelines for CPS deployment over NR-V2X Mode 2.

</details>


### [181] [An Experimental Assessment of the Spatial and Frequency Selectivity of Reconfigurable Intelligent Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26808)
*Cyrille Morin,Leonardo S Cardoso,Maxime Guillaud,Ahmad Shokair,Youssef Nasser,Amelie Hennequart,Geoffroy Lerosey*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验验证了可重构智能表面（RIS）在优化主通信链路时，会对邻近的次级链路产生显著干扰，即使两者使用不同频率，且该影响无法通过载波频率分离消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着RIS技术在5G/6G通信中的广泛应用，其配置优化可能无意中影响其他通信链路，需评估跨链路干扰风险以保障系统兼容性。

Method: 在FR1频段使用CorteXlab无线测试平台与Greenerwave RIS设备，设计三种场景实验：同频/异频主-次链路配置，测量次级链路的接收功率与信道相位变化。

Result: 实验显示RIS对次级链路的影响显著，即使次级链路频率超出RIS标称工作范围，且载波频率分离无法有效缓解干扰。

Conclusion: RIS配置优化需考虑对非目标链路的潜在干扰，未来系统设计应纳入跨链路干扰抑制机制。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) on radio links other than the one for which the RIS configuration is optimized. We consider three different scenarios in which a secondary communication link could be affected by a RIS whose configuration is optimized for a primary communication link operating in the vicinity, on the same or on different frequencies. This question is investigated experimentally in the FR1 band, using the CorteXlab radio testbed and a Greenerwave RIS. We show that the impact, in terms of received power and impact on the channel phase of the secondary link, is significant even outside of the nominal frequency range of the RIS, and is not mitigated by carrier frequency separation between the two communication links.

</details>


### [182] [Toward Agentic SysAdmin: Rethinking System Administration with AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26960)
*Gianmaria Frigo,Davide Saladino,Alberto Castagnaro,Francesco Marchiori,Denis Donadel,Luca Pajola,Mauro Conti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NetLLMeval是一个基于实时网络模拟的LLM网络管理任务评估框架，通过自动化基准测试解决了传统评估方法的扩展性问题，实验表明求解器设计对性能影响显著，开源框架支持可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM网络管理评估依赖静态参考输出或人工验证，无法适应真实网络状态多样性及多样化部署策略（从单体提示到智能体流水线），导致评估结果缺乏可扩展性和代表性。

Method: 提出NetLLMeval框架，通过实时网络模拟自动生成无干预的基准真值，开展24,000次全因子实验（覆盖10个基础模型、4种求解器架构、10类任务、6种拓扑复杂度递增的网络环境）。

Result: 求解器设计对准确率影响显著（14B开源模型正确率从0.43提升至0.88），本地部署模型在优化配置下可匹敌万亿参数前沿系统，验证了框架评估有效性。

Conclusion: NetLLMeval填补了LLM网络管理评估的空白，其开源框架为未来模型和求解器设计的可复现基准测试提供了标准化平台，推动LLM在网络运维领域的可靠应用。

Abstract: The growing complexity of computer networks, driven by cloud-native architectures, heterogeneous devices, and distributed systems, places increasing pressure on network administrators who must simultaneously manage configuration, troubleshooting, and security under tight operational constraints. Large Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as a promising tool to assist and partially automate these tasks, yet their systematic evaluation in networking scenarios remains an open challenge. Existing benchmarks rely on static reference outputs or manual expert validation, neither of which scales to the diversity of real-world network states or to the variety of orchestration strategies -- from monolithic prompting to fully agentic pipelines --through which LLMs are increasingly deployed.
  In this paper, we present NetLLMeval, a framework for automatically evaluating LLM-based systems on network administration tasks by leveraging live network emulation to derive ground truth without human intervention. Through a full-factorial study of 24000 runs spanning 10 foundation models, 4 solver architectures, 10 task types, and 6 network topologies of increasing complexity, we show that solver design has a great impact on accuracy -- lifting a 14B open-weight model from 0.43 to 0.88 correctness -- and that such locally-deployable models can match trillion-parameter frontier systems under the right configuration. NetLLMeval is released open-source to support reproducible benchmarking of future models and solver designs.

</details>


### [183] [Maximum Achievable Burst Size in All-Optical Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27050)
*Thomas Roethig,Sleman Mouammar,Ítalo Brasileiro,Soheil Hosseini,Admela Jukan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究分析了不同LEO星座下全光卫星网络的最大突发大小，发现基于WDM的光突发交换网络在100 Gbps上行链路下，高轨LEO支持500 MB，低轨LEO支持600 MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升全光卫星网络的突发数据传输效率，优化不同轨道高度下的网络性能。

Method: 采用WDM-based optical burst switching技术，在100 Gbps上行链路容量下测试不同LEO星座的突发大小。

Result: 高轨LEO星座最大突发大小为500 MB，低轨LEO星座达到600 MB。

Conclusion: 轨道高度显著影响突发传输能力，低轨LEO因更短延迟和更高带宽利用率可实现更大突发规模。

Abstract: We analyze the maximum burst size achievable in all-optical satellite networks across different constellations. With a 100 Gbps uplink capacity, a WDM-based optical burst switching network supports burst sizes of up to 500 MB in high-altitude LEO constellations and 600 MB in low-altitude LEO constellations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Unsupervised Memory-Enhanced Video Transformers: Obstacle Detection for Autonomous Agricultural Rover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26151)
*Théo Biardeau,Anne-Sophie Capelle-Laizé,Salwan Alwan,David Helbert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无监督视频异常检测方法VMTAD，通过Transformer与记忆模块处理动态农业场景，在油菜数据集实现0.973检测AUC和0.997分割AUC，轻量版达14ms实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LiDAR无法检测植物冠层下障碍物，监督学习方法对未见障碍物泛化性差，动态移动场景下无监督异常检测效果不足。

Method: 设计VMTAD架构：基于Transformer的编码器处理时序帧特征，记忆模块整合历史帧编码表示以建模动态场景，仅需正常操作数据无监督训练。

Result: 在Grillion无人车测试中，油菜数据集检测AUC达0.973、分割AUC达0.997；轻量版实现14ms推理延迟，满足安全制动距离要求。

Conclusion: VMTAD有效解决农业动态场景障碍物检测难题，为自动驾驶农机提供高精度实时安全保障。

Abstract: While autonomous rovers have become indispensable to precision farming, achieving consistent operational safety remains a critical challenge. Conventional safety sensors, such as LiDAR, fail to detect obstacles positioned below the plant canopy, posing a significant risk. While camera-based supervised learning methods can detect common objects, they perform poorly when faced with obstacles that were not present in their training data. Actual unsupervised anomaly detection offers a solution by learning the normal visual patterns of an environment, but often fails for the dynamic scenes captured by a moving rover.\\ This paper introduces Video Memory Transformers for Anomaly Detection (VMTAD), a fully unsupervised method designed for real-time obstacle detection in dynamic agricultural scenes. VMTAD utilizes a transformer-driven architecture augmented with a dedicated memory module. This memory module leverages temporal context by processing encoded representations of preceding frames. This approach enables the system to effectively address the dynamic context caused by the robot's movement. The model is trained using only images that represent normal operation, requiring no data labels.\\ VMTAD was rigorously evaluated on the 'Grillion' agricultural rover. On a challenging rapeseed dataset, VMTAD achieved state-of-the-art performance, reaching a 0.973 detection and 0.997 segmentation Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve. A lightweight variant provides an optimal balance of high accuracy and real-time inference (14 ms), which is critical for safety, as confirmed by our analysis of the rover's total stopping distance.

</details>


### [185] [Reinforcement Learning Enables Autonomous Microrobot Navigation and Intervention in Simulated Blood Capillaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26154)
